0211: async function runCertificateOperation(certificate: ManagedCertificate) {
0212:   const renewing = Boolean(certificate.not_after)
0213:   const challengeNotice = certificate.challenge_type === 'dns-01' ? '将通过 Cloudflare DNS 自动验证，不占用节点端口。' : '将先检查域名全部 A/AAAA 地址的公网 80 端口，再由现有 Webroot 提供挑战文件。'
0214:   if (!await confirmAction({ title: renewing ? '立即续期证书？' : '开始申请证书？', message: `${challengeNotice} 成功后${certificate.usage_count ? '会重新发布绑定协议的完整 Zero 配置' : '可在协议服务中绑定使用'}。`, confirmText: renewing ? '开始续期' : '开始申请' })) return
0215:   operatingID.value = certificate.id; error.value = ''; message.value = ''
0216:   try {
0214:   if (!await confirmAction({ title: renewing ? '立即续期证书？' : '开始申请证书？', message: `${challengeNotice} 成功后${certificate.usage_count ? '会重新发布绑定协议的完整 Zero 配置' : '可在协议服务中绑定使用'}。`, confirmText: renewing ? '开始续期' : '开始申请' })) return
0215:   operatingID.value = certificate.id; error.value = ''; message.value = ''
0216:   try {
0217:     if (renewing) await renewManagedCertificate(certificate.id)
0218:     else await issueManagedCertificate(certificate.id)
0219:     message.value = `${renewing ? '续期' : '申请'}操作已启动。`
0220:     await refresh()
0221:   } catch (cause: any) { error.value = cause?.response?.data?.message || '证书操作启动失败。' }
0222:   finally { operatingID.value = 0 }
0223: }
0224: async function removeCertificate(certificate: ManagedCertificate) {
0225:   if (!await confirmAction({
0226:     title: '删除托管证书？',
0227:     message: `将删除“${certificate.name}”的面板记录；签发/续期历史和节点上的证书文件会保留，避免破坏审计记录或误删仍被其他服务使用的文件。`,
0228:     confirmText: '确认删除',
0229:     tone: 'danger',
0230:   })) return
0216:   try {
0217:     if (renewing) await renewManagedCertificate(certificate.id)
0218:     else await issueManagedCertificate(certificate.id)
0219:     message.value = `${renewing ? '续期' : '申请'}操作已启动。`
0220:     await refresh()
0221:   } catch (cause: any) { error.value = cause?.response?.data?.message || '证书操作启动失败。' }
0222:   finally { operatingID.value = 0 }
0223: }

0228:     confirmText: '确认删除',
0229:     tone: 'danger',
0230:   })) return

0440: const kernelStatusLabel = computed(() => ({
0441:   unknown: '未检测',
0442:   not_installed: '未安装',
0443:   healthy: '健康',
0444:   degraded: '运行异常',
0445:   failed: '操作失败',
0446:   unsupported: '制品不兼容',
0447:   publishing: '配置发布中',
0448:   apply_failed: '配置应用失败',
0449: } as Record<string, string>)[kernelState.value?.status || 'unknown'] || formatUnknownValue('状态', kernelState.value?.status))
0450: const kernelDescription = computed(() => kernelState.value?.installed_version ? `Zero ${kernelState.value.installed_version} · ${serviceLabel(kernelState.value.service_status)}` : kernelStatusLabel.value)
0451: const reconcileButtonLabel = computed(() => kernelState.value?.status === 'unsupported' ? '当前制品不兼容' : kernelState.value?.status === 'not_installed' ? '自动安装' : kernelState.value?.recommended_action === 'configure' ? '应用配置' : '安装 / 升级 / 修复')
0452: function lifecycleLabel(value?: string) { return ({ active: '正常', maintenance: '维护', retired: '退役' } as Record<string, string>)[value || ''] || formatUnknownValue('状态', value) }

0723: async function rotateReport(node: any) { if (node.traffic_secret_prefix && !await confirmAction({ title: '轮换流量凭证', message: '轮换后旧流量上报凭证会立即失效。', confirmText: '确认轮换', tone: 'danger' })) return; detailError.value = ''; try { const result = await rotateNodeReportCredential(node.id); Object.assign(secretModal, { title: '流量上报凭证', value: result.secret, copied: false, nodeID: node.id }); await refreshSelectedNodeAfterCredentialChange(node.id, { traffic_secret_prefix: result.secret_prefix, traffic_secret_revoked_at: undefined }) } catch (e: any) { detailError.value = e?.response?.data?.message || '流量上报凭证操作失败。' } }
0724: async function revokeReport(node: any) { if (!await confirmAction({ title: '吊销流量凭证', message: '吊销后节点将无法继续提交可信流量记录。', confirmText: '确认吊销', tone: 'danger' })) return; detailError.value = ''; detailMessage.value = ''; try { await revokeNodeReportCredential(node.id); detailMessage.value = '流量上报凭证已吊销。'; await refreshSelectedNodeAfterCredentialChange(node.id, { traffic_secret_revoked_at: new Date().toISOString() }) } catch (e: any) { detailError.value = e?.response?.data?.message || '吊销失败。' } }
0725: async function copySecret() { try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(secretModal.value); secretModal.copied = true } catch { secretModal.copied = false } }
0726: 
0727: watch([() => selectedNode.value?.id, detailSection, nodeProtocolOffset, nodeProtocolLimit], ([id, section], [previousID]) => {
0728:   if (id !== previousID) {
0729:     kernelState.value = null
0730:     kernelOperations.value = []
0731:     nodeEndpoints.value = []
0732:     nodeLoad.value = null
0733:     nodeLoadError.value = ''
0734:     nodeProtocolTotal.value = 0
0789: })

0172: async function syncURL(replace = false) { const page = Math.floor(offset.value / limit.value) + 1, eventPage = Math.floor(paymentEventOffset.value / paymentEventLimit.value) + 1; const location = { query: { ...preserveAdminReturnTo(route.query.return_to), ...(queryFilter.value ? { q: queryFilter.value } : {}), ...(statusFilter.value ? { status: statusFilter.value } : {}), ...(orderTypeFilter.value ? { order_type: orderTypeFilter.value } : {}), ...(userFilter.value ? { user_id: userFilter.value } : {}), ...(createdFrom.value ? { created_from: createdFrom.value } : {}), ...(createdTo.value ? { created_to: createdTo.value } : {}), ...(page > 1 ? { page: String(page) } : {}), ...(limit.value !== 50 ? { limit: String(limit.value) } : {}), ...(detailID.value ? { order: String(detailID.value) } : {}), ...(detailID.value && eventPage > 1 ? { event_page: String(eventPage) } : {}), ...(detailID.value && paymentEventLimit.value !== 25 ? { event_limit: String(paymentEventLimit.value) } : {}) } }; await (replace ? router.replace(location) : router.push(location)) }
0173: async function applyFilters() { offset.value = 0; await syncURL(); await load() }
0174: async function clearFilters() { queryFilter.value = ''; statusFilter.value = ''; orderTypeFilter.value = ''; userFilter.value = ''; createdFrom.value = ''; createdTo.value = ''; offset.value = 0; await syncURL(); await load() }
0175: async function changePage(value: { offset: number; limit: number }) { offset.value = value.offset; limit.value = value.limit; await syncURL(); await load() }
0176: function requestAction(kind: 'pay' | 'cancel', item: OrderItem) { actionKind.value = kind; actionTarget.value = item; confirmOpen.value = true }
0177: async function executeAction() { if (!actionTarget.value) return; saving.value = true; error.value = ''; message.value = ''; try { if (actionKind.value === 'pay') await markOrderPaid(actionTarget.value.id); else await cancelOrder(actionTarget.value.id, true); message.value = actionKind.value === 'pay' ? `订单 #${actionTarget.value.id} 已确认收款。` : `订单 #${actionTarget.value.id} 已取消。`; confirmOpen.value = false; await load(); if (detailID.value === actionTarget.value.id) { selectedOrder.value = null; await syncDetailFromRoute() } } catch (e: any) { error.value = e?.response?.data?.message || '订单操作失败。' } finally { saving.value = false } }
0178: async function openDetail(id: number) { const { event_page: _eventPage, event_limit: _eventLimit, ...query } = route.query; paymentEventOffset.value = 0; paymentEventLimit.value = 25; await router.push({ query: { ...query, order: String(id) } }) }
0179: async function closeDetail() { detailController?.abort(); detailID.value = 0; selectedOrder.value = null; detailError.value = ''; paymentEvents.value = []; const { order: _order, event_page: _eventPage, event_limit: _eventLimit, ...query } = route.query; await router.push({ query }) }
0180: async function changePaymentEventPage(value: { offset: number; limit: number }) { paymentEventOffset.value = value.offset; paymentEventLimit.value = value.limit; await syncURL(); await loadPaymentEvents() }
0181: async function syncDetailFromRoute() {

0186:   }

0094:   }),
0095: )
0080: const siteState = useDirtyForm(() => form)
0081: const hiddenSiteKeys = new Set(['site_name', 'site_url', 'register_switch'])
0082: const operationalConfigs = computed(() => configs.value.filter(item => !hiddenSiteKeys.has(item.config_key)).sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id))
0083: const emailConfigs = computed(() => operationalConfigs.value.filter(item => /smtp|email/i.test(item.config_key)))
0084: const otherConfigs = computed(() => operationalConfigs.value.filter(item => !/smtp|email/i.test(item.config_key)))
0085: const configChanges = computed(() => operationalConfigs.value.filter(configDirty).length)
0086: const pageDirty = computed(() => siteState.dirty.value || configChanges.value > 0)
0087: useUnsavedChangesGuard(
0088:   () => pageDirty.value,
0089:   () => confirmAction({
0090:     title: '离开系统设置？',
0091:     message: `当前有 ${configChanges.value + (siteState.dirty.value ? 1 : 0)} 组修改尚未保存。`,
0092:     confirmText: '放弃修改',
0093:     tone: 'danger',
0094:   }),
0095: )
0096:

0016:     if (node.type === Text) {
0017:       if (String(node.children || '').trim()) result.push(node)
0018:       continue
